Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了皮埃尔·奥格天文台对由磁矩诱导的活性中微子与重惰性中微子之间跃迁所引发的标准模型之外物理现象的敏感性，通过分析超高能中微子探测率的变化，给出了在1 TeV–100 TeV惰性中微子质量范围内、90%置信水平下的跃迁磁矩限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准模型之外的新物理，特别是通过磁偶极门户相互作用引起的活性与重惰性中微子之间的跃迁，并利用超高能中微子观测数据对其参数进行约束。

Method: 基于皮埃尔·奥格天文台对下向和地平线掠射中微子的探测能力，建模分析磁矩诱导跃迁对中微子-核子截面的影响，并结合未观测到超高能中微子候选事件的数据，推导出跃迁磁矩的90%置信度限制。

Result: 在1 TeV–100 TeV惰性中微子质量范围内，得到依赖于中微子通量模型的跃迁磁矩上限；在所假设通量场景下，该限制拓展至此前未被探测的参数空间。

Conclusion: 皮埃尔·奥格天文台可有效约束磁偶极门户模型中的中微子跃迁磁矩，为重惰性中微子新物理提供新的实验探针。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of the Pierre Auger Observatory to physics beyond the Standard Model arising from magnetic-moment-induced transitions between active and heavy sterile neutrinos. Such dipole portal interactions can enhance neutrino-nucleon cross sections above a kinematic threshold set by the sterile neutrino mass, leading to observable modifications of neutrino detection rates at ultrahigh energies (UHE). We estimate the impact of these interactions on both down-going and Earth-skimming neutrino detection channels, the contrasting responses of which enable discrimination between an enhanced neutrino flux and a modified interaction cross section. Using the non-observation of UHE neutrino candidates, we derive neutrino-flux-dependent constraints with 90% confidence-level on the transition magnetic moment for sterile neutrino masses in the range 1 TeV-100 TeV. Under the assumed flux scenarios, the resulting flavor-independent limits extend existing bounds into previously unexplored parameter space.

</details>


### [119] [Magnetic field modifications to the pion-quark vertex in the Linear Sigma Model with quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01701)
*Alejandro Ayala,Flávia Fialho,Adrián Lara,Ana Mizher,Javier Rendón*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用线性σ模型（含夸克）研究了恒定均匀磁场对夸克-中性π介子顶点的修正，发现即使在树图阶，由于平移不变性破缺，需在坐标空间中用Ritus波函数而非平面波描述夸克；同时计算了一圈修正，并对任意朗道能级及最低朗道能级情形分别给出结果，且用Schwinger固有时间方法验证了最低朗道能级下的一圈结果一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强磁场下QCD低能有效理论中夸克与中性π介子相互作用顶点的修改，以理解磁场对强子结构和相互作用的影响。

Method: 采用含夸克的线性σ模型作为QCD低能有效理论，在恒定均匀磁场下，利用Ritus波函数在坐标空间中计算树图和一圈阶的顶点修正；并用Schwinger固有时间方法对最低朗道能级情形进行交叉验证。

Result: 发现磁场导致顶点在树图阶即发生修正；一圈修正结果对任意朗道能级成立，并在最低朗道能级情形下通过两种方法（Ritus形式论与Schwinger方法）获得一致结果。

Conclusion: 磁场显著改变夸克-π介子耦合，即使在树图阶亦不可忽略；Ritus波函数是处理磁场中非平移不变系统的合适工具，且不同方法间结果自洽，增强了结论可靠性。

Abstract: We compute the modification to the quark-neutral pion vertex, induced by a constant and uniform magnetic field, using the Linear Sigma Model with quarks as a low energy effective theory of QCD. The vertex is modified even at tree-level since, due to the loss of translational invariance, the calculation should be carried out in configuration space, with the quark described by Ritus wave functions instead of plane waves. We also compute the one-loop vertex modification. These modifications are found for arbitrary Landau levels occupied by the quark. We illustrate the result for the case where the quark occupies the lowest Landau level. To check the result, we also compute the one-loop vertex modification using the Schwinger proper-time method with the quark occupying the lowest Landau level and find the same result as in the case where the Ritus formalism is used.

</details>


### [120] [Jet-by-jet energy correlators as stochastic probes of the parton-to-hadron transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01764)
*Jingyu Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了能量-能量关联器（EEC）在单个喷注层面的分布与协方差特性，发现其角壳权重具有稀疏性与强非高斯性，协方差呈现跨壳结构；该结构对强子化过程敏感，能提供平均谱所缺失的 parton→hadron 转变信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEC仅报告系综平均，丢失喷注间角区域联合涨落信息；需从单喷注层面提取EEC的统计结构以探测强子化动力学。

Method: 对每个喷注保留分箱二点关联器，分析其角壳权重的分布与协方差；采用Pythia/Herwig生成parton/hadron级样本，比较不同生成器、半径和动量配置下的协方差特征（如迹、邻壳相关性、特征位置）。

Result: 壳变量高度稀疏且非高斯；协方差含显著跨壳结构；回归5个喷注全局观测量仅保留84–92%协方差迹；shuffling无法复现离对角相关；hadron-level协方差迹减小、邻壳相关增强，且变化模式因生成器而异。

Conclusion: 喷注级EEC协方差是探测强子化过程的新探针，其角依赖性和生成器差异表明它携带平均EEC谱中不存在的关键强子化信息。

Abstract: Energy-energy correlators are usually reported as ensemble averages and therefore do not specify how different angular regions fluctuate together from jet to jet. We retain the binned two-point correlator for each jet and study the distribution and covariance of its angular-shell weights. In a high-statistics Pythia sample, the shell variables are sparse and strongly non-Gaussian, and their covariance contains nonzero cross-shell structure spread over many modes. Regressing each shell on five jet-level summaries leaves 84-92% of the covariance trace, while shell-wise permutation and constituent-angle shuffling do not reproduce the observed off-diagonal correlations. We then compare separately generated parton- and hadron-level samples in Pythia and Herwig. Across 24 generator-radius-momentum configurations, the hadron-level covariance trace is smaller and the average neighboring-shell correlation over 0.30 <= r/R < 1 is larger. The size and angular dependence of the change differ between the generators, and a characteristic-position analysis does not identify a common scale. Jet-by-jet EEC covariance thus provides information on the parton-to-hadron transition that is absent from the mean spectrum.

</details>


### [121] [Odderon exchange in high-energy $K^0_S$ regeneration at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01768)
*P. Filip,M. Taševský,V. A. Khoze,R. Pasechnik,M. G. Ryskin,B. G. Zakharov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新探讨了通过高能中性K介子再生探测Odderon交换的可能性，重点分析了在LHC前向区域使用不同材料再生器（如C、Cu、Pb）对TeV量级K⁰_L→K⁰_S转换的可行性，并区分了相干前向与非前向（衍射）再生两种机制；指出电磁背景和初级K⁰_S污染是主要挑战，提出双再生器减法策略以压制背景并提升Odderon信号可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Odderon作为强相互作用中C-奇数的胶子态，其存在尚未被明确实验证实；中性K介子再生对C-奇数交换敏感，是潜在探针，但此前未在TeV能区结合LHC前向物理条件系统评估。

Method: 理论建模与数值估算相结合：1）复现并拓展原始相干前向再生模型，计入实际再生器材料（C/Cu/Pb）、中性K介子衰减及电磁背景（光子交换）；2）分析非前向（衍射）再生在0.2–0.8 TeV能区的信噪比，量化Odderon、Pomeron-Odderon cut、ω-Reggeon及光子交换等振幅贡献，并评估中子诱导本底与非弹性Regge背景的影响；3）提出主动式双再生器减法方案以抑制系统本底。

Result: 1）相干前向再生中，Odderon相位可导致K⁰→π⁰π⁰顶点分布可观测畸变，但受初级K⁰_S存活率与电磁C=-1振幅严重干扰，难以干净提取Odderon信号；2）非前向再生在较低能量（0.2–0.8 TeV）下天然抑制K⁰_S污染，Odderon信号相对增强，但中子诱导的中性奇异粒子产生及非弹性Regge背景成为主导限制；3）需约10²–10³倍背景压制，双再生器策略可望实现该目标。

Conclusion: TeV能区中性K介子再生是探测Odderon的可行新途径，尤其非前向模式更具潜力；但需精密实验设计（如双再生器、靶材选择、动量分辨与本底刻度）以克服关键物理与技术限制；该研究为LHC前向实验（如FASERν、SND@LHC后续升级）提供了理论依据与可行性框架。

Abstract: We revisit the possibility of detecting the Odderon exchange through high-energy neutral-kaon regeneration, focusing on the in-matter $K^0_L\to K^0_S$ conversion of $(\sim\!0.2-2)$~TeV $K^0_L$ mesons originating from $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$~TeV, and on the practical constraints of realizing such a measurement in the very-forward region of an LHC interaction point. The analysis has two complementary parts. First, we reproduce the original coherent-forward-regeneration estimates for a liquid-hydrogen regenerator and extend them to realistic C, Cu and Pb regenerators of an LHC-compatible geometry, including neutral-kaon attenuation. We show that an Odderon-induced regeneration phase produces a measurable distortion of the $K^0\toπ^0π^0$ decay-vertex distribution at TeV kaon energies, but that the survival of primary $K^0_S$ mesons from the interaction point imposes severe baseline requirements, while a competing electromagnetic $C=-1$ (photon-exchange) amplitude limits the interpretation of the coherent mode as a clean Odderon measurement. Second, we examine non-forward (diffractive) regeneration at lower kaon energies of $0.2-0.8$~TeV, where the primary-$K^0_S$ contamination is naturally suppressed, and estimate the competing Odderon, Pomeron--Odderon-cut, $ω$-Reggeon and photon-exchange contributions to the regeneration amplitude. We identify neutron-induced neutral-only strangeness production and inelastic Regge backgrounds as the dominant limitations, quantify the background suppression required for an observable Odderon signal, and formulate the ingredients of an active double-regenerator subtraction strategy.

</details>


### [122] [Gluon condensate effects on heavy quarkonium spectral functions and thermal dissociation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01801)
*Fei Wang,Zi-qiang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过改进的软墙AdS/QCD模型，研究胶子凝聚态对重矢量介子（如粲偶素和底偶素）热谱函数及熔解行为的影响，发现胶子凝聚态能抑制热致离解、稳定夸克偶素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究胶子凝聚态如何影响重矢量介子在高温下的谱函数与熔解行为，以理解其在夸克-胶子等离子体中的稳定性机制。

Method: 基于改进的软墙AdS/QCD模型，引入含胶子凝聚态反作用的稀释子黑洞几何，在有限温度下数值计算谱共振峰随温度和胶子凝聚强度的演化。

Result: 温度升高导致谱峰展宽与压制，表征介质内离解；而更强的胶子凝聚显著减缓展宽、增强基态与激发态谱权重。

Conclusion: 胶子凝聚态阻碍热致离解，提升重夸克偶素在夸克-胶子等离子体中的稳定性，为该效应提供了新的全息证据。

Abstract: We investigate how the gluon condensate modifies the thermal spectral functions and melting patterns of heavy vector mesons, specifically charmonium and bottomonium, using an improved soft-wall AdS/QCD model. The framework is extended to finite temperature via a dilaton black hole geometry that consistently incorporates the backreaction from the gluon condensate. We numerically track the evolution of spectral resonance peaks as functions of both temperature and gluon condensate strength. Our calculations reveal that increasing temperature systematically broadens and suppresses spectral peaks, signaling in-medium dissociation. In contrast, a stronger gluon condensate considerably mitigates peak broadening and enhances the spectral weight of both ground and excited states. This behavior indicates that the gluon condensate hinders thermal dissociation, thereby stabilizing heavy quarkonia within the quark-gluon plasma. These findings are consistent with existing studies and provide new holographic evidence for the stabilising role of the gluon condensate from the perspective of thermal spectral functions.

</details>


### [123] [Measuring the trilinear Higgs self-coupling in Higgs boson pair production at multi-TeV muon colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01888)
*Kingman Cheung,Jae Sik Lee,Soojin Lee,Chen Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了多TeV缪子对撞机在Higgs双产生过程中测量三线性Higgs自耦合修饰因子κ₃的灵敏度，通过bb̄bb̄末态结合先进机器学习方法（如SPANet配对网络、能量流拓扑分析及双分类器）实现信号提取，在10 TeV下达到百分之几的精度，显著优于HL-LHC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三线性Higgs自耦合决定Higgs势的形状，其精确测量是未来对撞机的核心目标之一。

Method: 利用3 TeV（1 ab⁻¹）和10 TeV（10 ab⁻¹）缪子对撞机的Higgs双产生（VBF过程，bb̄bb̄末态），划分分辨与增强区域，结合SPANet型监督配对网络、事件能量流的拓扑数据分析，以及两个专用分类器D_HH和D_κ₃，通过二维似然拟合提取κ₃。

Result: 在68%置信水平下，3 TeV时κ₃区间为0.80–1.29，10 TeV时为0.96–1.05；后者达百分之几精度，远超HL-LHC预期精度。

Conclusion: 10 TeV缪子对撞机有望以统计极限精度精确测定Higgs三线性自耦合，确立其作为探索电弱对称性破缺机制的关键工具。

Abstract: The trilinear Higgs self-coupling determines the shape of the Higgs potential, and its measurement is a central goal of future colliders. We assess the sensitivity of multi-TeV muon colliders to the coupling modifier $κ_3$ in Higgs boson pair production via vector boson fusion, using the $b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$ final state at $\sqrt{s}=3$ TeV with $1$ ab$^{-1}$ and at $10$ TeV with $10$ ab$^{-1}$. Events are analyzed in two complementary regions, a resolved region with four jets and a boosted region with two large-radius jets. To extract the signal from backgrounds a few orders of magnitude larger, we combine a supervised jet-to-Higgs pairing network based on the SPANet approach, topological data analysis of the event energy flow, and two dedicated classifiers, $D_{\rm HH}$ for the signal-to-background separation and $D_{κ_3}$ for the $κ_3$ shape information. The coupling is extracted from a two-dimensional likelihood fit to the distribution of the two classifier outputs. Combining the two regions, we obtain $0.80<κ_3<1.29$ at $3$ TeV and $0.96<κ_3<1.05$ at $10$ TeV at $68\%$ confidence level. The $10$ TeV determination reaches the few-percent level in this statistics-limited projection, which surpasses by a large amount the precision projected for the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [124] [Thermal Leptogenesis in the BNT Model of Neutrino Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01890)
*P. S. Bhupal Dev,Srubabati Goswami,Debashis Pachhar,Drona Vatsyayan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Babu-Nandi-Tavartkiladze（BNT）模型中的中微子质量与热轻子生成机制，该模型包含一个标量四重态Φ和一对矢量类费米子三重态Σ；中微子质量通过树图维度7算子和单圈维度5算子产生，可在TeV能标下自然实现亚eV质量；热轻子生成在分层谱下需Σ质量≥10⁷ GeV，但在准简并情形下借助CP不对称性的共振增强可降至TeV量级，从而兼顾理论自洽与实验可检验性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在TeV能标新物理框架下同时解释中微子小质量与宇宙正反物质不对称（通过热轻子生成），并确保模型具有对撞机可检验性。

Method: 基于BNT模型（含标量四重态Φ与矢量类费米子三重态Σ），系统计算中微子质量生成机制（树图维度7与单圈维度5算子），并分析热轻子生成中CP不对称性、衰变动力学及共振效应，尤其对比分层谱与准简并谱下的轻子生成温度阈值。

Result: 中微子质量可在O(TeV)新物理能标下自然实现亚eV量级；热轻子生成在分层谱下要求M_Σ ≳ 10⁷ GeV，而在准简并谱下因共振增强CP不对称性，成功轻子生成可低至O(TeV)能标。

Conclusion: BNT模型不仅能自然解释中微子质量，还能在TeV能标实现可行的热轻子生成，显著提升其现象学意义与实验可检验性，为对撞机探索重子不对称起源提供了新路径。

Abstract: We investigate neutrino mass and thermal leptogenesis in the Babu-Nandi-Tavartkiladze (BNT) model featuring a scalar quadruplet ($Φ$) and a pair of vector-like fermion triplets ($Σ$). In this framework, neutrino masses are generated via an effective dimension-7 operator $LLHH(H^{\dagger}H)/Λ^3$ at the tree level and via the dimension-5 operator $LLHH/Λ$ at the one-loop level. It naturally accommodates sub-eV neutrino masses even if the new physics scale $Λ$ is $\mathcal{O}(\rm TeV)$, thus making the model a compelling target for experimental searches. We explore the viability of thermal leptogenesis in this model, which is distinct from the canonical seesaw-based leptogenesis due to the presence of vector-like fermions. We find that leptogenesis is viable for $M_Σ\gtrsim 10^{7}$ GeV for a hierarchical spectrum of fermion triplets. However, in the quasi-degenerate regime, resonant enhancement of the $CP$ asymmetry lowers this scale down to $\mathcal{O}({\rm TeV})$, reconciling successful leptogenesis with the originally motivated TeV-scale phenomenology and testability of the model at colliders.

</details>


### [125] [A quality-coupling relation in chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$ axion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01897)
*Yu-Cheng Qiu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过规范对称性（U(1)_{B-L}）保护QCD轴子质量的新机制，其中PQ对称性是偶然的，从而避免了PQ荷分配的模糊性；高轴子质量要求轴子与U(1)_{B-L}规范玻色子（即带电Majoron）具有强反常耦合，并由此导出轴子与标准模型费米子耦合的‘质量下限’，拓展了可探测的轴子参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决QCD轴子质量保护问题，避免传统PQ对称性人为设定带来的电荷分配模糊性，同时提升轴子的可检验性。

Method: 构建基于手征U(1)_{B-L}规范对称性的模型，引入两个复标量场，其真空期望值破缺该对称性，产生一个被规范玻色子吃掉的相位和一个作为QCD轴子的剩余相位；利用偶然PQ对称性确保轴子质量稳定，并分析反常耦合导致的轴子-费米子耦合下限。

Result: 发现高轴子质量自然导致轴子与U(1)_{B-L}规范玻色子的强反常耦合，并由此推导出轴子与标准模型费米子耦合的不可规避的最小值（‘质量下限’），从而扩展了实验可探测的轴子参数空间。

Conclusion: 基于U(1)_{B-L}规范对称性的偶然PQ机制为QCD轴子提供了稳健的质量保护，并增强了其在当前和未来实验中的可观测性。

Abstract: The quality of the QCD axion can be protected by a gauge symmetry. This work considers a gauged chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$. The vacuum expectation values of two complex scalars break $U(1)_{B-L}$. One linear combination of their two phase degrees of freedom is eaten by the $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson, while the other becomes the QCD axion. Since the PQ symmetry is accidental, there is no ambiguity in the PQ charge assignment. High axion quality implies a large anomalous coupling between the axion and the $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson (or gauged Majoron). A `quality floor', namely a minimal coupling determined by the axion quality, arises for the coupling between the axion and SM fermions, extending the parameter space of QCD axions toward improved testability.

</details>


### [126] [The $(2+1)$-dimensional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model at finite temperature, density, and magnetic field within the Functional Renormalization Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02280)
*Justin L. P. Mauldin,Dirk H. Rischke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用功能重整化群（FRG）方法，在局域势近似下研究了(2+1)维Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型在有限温、密度和磁场下的相图，发现了磁催化、维度约化、手征对称性破缺增强、一级相变、de Haas–van Alphen振荡及临界端点随磁场增强向高温移动等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联量子系统在极端条件（如强磁场、有限温密度）下的相结构与非平凡量子现象，特别是手征对称性破缺行为及其临界特性。

Method: 采用功能重整化群（FRG）方法，在局域势近似下结合流体动力学算法求解有效势的FRG流方程，以更深入红外区域。

Result: 发现强磁场导致磁催化、系统维度约化及手征对称性破缺增强；手征凝聚表现出多个一级相变、小磁场大化学势下的de Haas–van Alphen振荡，以及随磁场增大而向更高温度移动的临界端点。

Conclusion: FRG方法结合新数值算法可有效刻画(2+1)维强耦合模型在多参数调控下的丰富相结构，为理解二维狄拉克材料等实际体系提供理论支撑。

Abstract: We investigate the phase diagram of the (2+1)-dimensional Gross-Neveu-Yukawa (GNY) model at finite temperature, density, and magnetic field beyond mean-field, using the Functional Renormalization Group (FRG) in the local potential approximation. Large magnetic fields result in magnetic catalysis, a dimensional reduction of the system, and enhancement of chiral symmetry breaking. We employ a hydrodynamical algorithm to solve the FRG flow equation for the effective potential, which allows for go further into the infrared region than with previously used methods. We find that the chiral condensate exhibits non-trivial behavior in various regions of the phase diagram: several first-order phase transitions and de Haas -- van Alphen oscillations at small magnetic field and large chemical potential, as well as a critical endpoint which shifts to higher temperature with increasing magnetic field.

</details>

I will address issues important, for the last case, when Monte Carlo programs that simultaneously account for theoretical and experimental effects are necessary. To complete efforts, simultaneous effort in many specialities over years was necessary. The monumental projects of Bryan Lynn, Robin Stuart, Dima Bardin, Wolfgang Hollik and contributions of S. Jadach in the domain of precision physics need to be mentioned, they provide theoretical foundations. Incomplete lists of methodology domains and projects, usually left into references, appendices, and private notes include: (i) Phase space--> symmetries (ii) matrix element preparation --> factorizations (iii) program and development process design (iv) testing strategies (v) user interaction (vi) software tools (vii) partners and competitors.
  Usually we were publishing our own projects, let me mention incomplete lists of methodology domains and projects, which were usually left aside into references, appendices, and private notes: The work started on the basis of previous efforts which can be listed following names of the programs: (i) FOWL, (ii) GENRAP, (iii) Mustraal, (iv) Koralb, (v) Lesko, (vi) Tauola, (vii) KoralZ, (viii) Lumlog, (ix) Oldbab, (x) Bhlumi, (xi) Bhwide and (xii) KKMC. I will focus on some of these points. Others, hopefully, are covered in other talks. In particular I do not need to cover exponentiation and some issues of factorization; see contributions to the proceedings by B.F.L. Ward and A. Tapadar. Developments took years and did not follow a straight line; that is why there are simplifications, biases. Also a review of literature could not be completed.

Abstract: In this contribution I present the first exact calculation of the leading-colour two-loop QCD amplitude for the associated production of a top-anti-top pair and a W boson. I discuss strategies to address the complexity of the computation, which involves complicated analytic structures, such as nested square roots, elliptic functions, and expressions with a high degree of algebraic complexity. The final result is expressed in terms of a set of special functions, which are evaluated using the method of differential equations, and rational coefficients, evaluated via finite field techniques.

</details>

*Daniel Jadlovský,Andreas Sander,Lee Patrick,Jiří Krtička,Gemma González-Torà,Matheus Bernini-Peron*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文强调哈勃太空望远镜（HST）在红超巨星（RSG）双星系统研究中的不可替代性，尤其在紫外波段探测热伴星以完善轨道解和演化状态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着干涉与天体测量技术进步，现已能分辨RSG双星的轨道运动，但长波段观测被冷RSG主导，需HST紫外观测热伴星以补全关键参数。

Method: 结合HST高分辨率紫外观测能力与最新高角分辨率干涉/天体测量技术，对RSG+B双星系统开展观测分析。

Result: HST紫外观测可有效约束热伴星性质与速度，从而完善RSG双星轨道解与演化状态判断。

Conclusion: HST在当前及未来十年对理解大质量双星相互作用、物质转移及其与超新星关联方面仍至关重要。

Abstract: The Hubble Space Telescope (HST) has advanced our knowledge of stellar evolution thanks to its unique high-spectral resolution and high-sensitivity capabilities in the UV region, especially for hot massive stars. Within the field of red supergiants (RSGs), it has led to several major insights as well, such as, for example, characterizing the embedded hot companion in the VV Cephei system, the detection of surface mass ejection from Betelgeuse, and unveiling the rich population of RSG+B binaries in the Magellanic Clouds. We demonstrate that nowadays (and in the decade to come) we need HST more than ever, as for the first time, thanks to the significant advances of high-angular-resolution interferometric and astrometric techniques, we can resolve the orbital motion of stellar components in RSG systems. However, the long wavelengths are dominated by the cool RSGs. Thus, observing such companions in the UV with HST is the only way to constrain the properties and velocity of the hot companions, adding the required missing piece of information for constraining the orbital solution and evolutionary status of RSG systems. Such observations are critical for understanding the interaction and mass transfer in massive binary systems and their connection to the observed population of supernovae.

</details>


### [130] [An upgraded frequency-selectable laser source (FLS) calibrator for CMB bandpass characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00192)

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种新型频率可调激光源（FLS）校准器，用于提升宇宙微波背景（CMB）实验中仪器带通校准的精度，以克服现有傅里叶变换光谱仪（FTS）系统受限于系统误差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMB实验中仪器带通校准的不确定性限制了前景去除和光谱拟合精度，进而影响暴胀与星系团测量；当前使用的FTS系统受限于系统误差，亟需新型校准源。

Method: 研发并升级了频率可调激光源（FLS）校准器，包含可调频激光器与功率衰减系统，并对其性能进行表征。

Result: 完成了FLS校准器的升级、新激光源的表征，并制定了在微波仪器上的实地测试计划。

Conclusion: FLS校准器有望显著提升CMB实验带通校准精度，为高精度宇宙学测量提供关键技术支撑。

Abstract: One of the biggest challenges for Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) experiments comes from the uncertainty in instrument bandpass calibration. Uncertainties in bandpass can limit foreground removal and spectral fitting, which are critical for inflationary and galaxy cluster measurements. CMB experiments currently use Fourier Transform Spectrometers (FTSes) to measure instrument bandpasses. However, FTS systems are currently systematics-limited, so significant improvements in bandpass measurements require novel calibrators. To this end, we developed a Frequency-selectable Laser Source (FLS) calibrator, which uses a laser with adjustable frequency coupled to a system that allows for laser power attenuation. Following initial testing with the first FLS prototype, we developed an upgraded version of the calibrator with improved performance. We present the upgrades to the FLS calibrator, the characterization of the upgraded calibrator and new laser source, and plans for testing with microwave instruments in the field.

</details>

Method: 构建端到端光子传输模拟框架，整合波长相关系统效率、ESO SkyCalc天空背景、多成分噪声模型；基于MPDAF开发pyetc_wst库，支持多种源光谱与空间分布；提供四种运行模式及多接口（Web/REST/CLI）。

Result: 实现了功能完备的ETC，可输出波长相关的信噪比、源/天空光子数、各噪声组分分解及模拟提取光谱，已支持交互式使用与批量巡天设计。

Conclusion: 该ETC是WST概念设计的关键支撑工具，具备高保真度、灵活性与易用性，为未来光谱巡天的可行性分析与优化提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive Exposure Time Calculator (ETC) developed for the Wide-field Spectroscopic Telescope (WST) concept. The WST, currently in its conceptual phase, is designed as a next-generation large spectroscopic survey facility featuring three complementary observing modes: an Integral Field Spectrograph (IFS) covering 370-930 nm at R of about 4800; a high-resolution Multi-Object Spectrograph (MOS-HR) with four bands at R of about 40000; and a low-resolution Multi-Object Spectrograph (MOS-LR) with four channels at R of about 3800-4900. The ETC simulates the complete photon-propagation path from astronomical source to detector, incorporating wavelength-dependent system throughput (telescope transmission, instrumental optics, detector quantum efficiency), accurate sky background via ESO SkyCalc integration, and a comprehensive noise treatment (photon noise, sky background, read-out noise, dark current). The computational core is implemented as the "pyetc_wst" Python library built on the MPDAF framework, supporting multiple target spectral energy distributions (stellar templates, blackbody, power-law, emission lines, and user-uploaded spectra with arbitrary redshift) and spatial morphologies (point sources and Sersic extended profiles). Four operational modes enable flexible exposure-time optimization. Full spectral outputs include wavelength-dependent signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), source and sky photon counts, noise decomposition by component, and simulated extracted spectra. An interactive web interface, together with a REST API and a command-line tool, complete the user experience and enable batch survey-design workflows.

</details>


### [132] [Calibration of MEMS DM actuator gains using a Zernike wavefront sensor on the HiCAT testbed and implications for Habitable Worlds Observatory operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00221)
*Sarah Steiger,Raphaël Pourcelot,Rémi Soummer,Emiel H. Por,Laurent Pueyo,Iva Laginja,Nathan Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Zernike波前传感器（ZWFS）的可变形镜（DM）执行器增益校准方法，用于提升高对比度成像中‘暗区挖掘’效率，实验验证了该方法在HiCAT测试平台上的有效性，并展示了其对对比度性能的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现直接成像冷或反射光行星所需的超高对比度（<1e-8），必须精确校准可变形镜（DM）执行器的增益；而DM增益随行程变化，需快速、原位更新增益图以维持暗区挖掘效率。

Method: 在STScI的HiCAT测试平台上，利用Fizeau干涉仪和Zernike波前传感器（ZWFS）对Boston Micromachines 952单元MEMS DM进行增益图校准；ZWFS方法包括局部线性拟合与更精确但计算量更大的二次拟合形式化方法。

Result: 成功获得高精度DM增益图，提升了暗区挖掘效率；结合14至16位控制电子系统，实现了对比度性能提升。

Conclusion: ZWFS是一种高效、高灵敏（皮米级）的原位DM增益校准工具，适用于未来空间望远镜（如HWO、Roman CGI）的高对比度成像任务，能降低系统开销并缓解对平台稳定性的严苛要求。

Abstract: Deformable mirrors (DMs) are a key component of coronagraph instruments performing adaptive optics on the ground, and for future space observatories such as HWO and Roman CGI. Here, they will be used as part of the wavefront sensing and control system to "dig a dark zone'' - remove residual stellar light to create a high-contrast region in the focal plane where faint companions can be detected. To reach the deep contrasts needed to directly image cool or reflected light planets (<1e-8) accurate calibration of the DM actuator gain is essential as picometer differences between the expected and realized DM surface can significantly degrade dark zone (DZ) digging efficiency. This increases the overheads needed to achieve a DZ and critically places more stringent requirements on observatory stability. Furthermore, DM gain varies with actuator stroke, necessitating rapid, in situ gain map recalculations to maintain DZ digging efficiency over time. Zernike wavefront sensors (ZWFS) are well-suited for this task as they efficiently provide picometer-level sensitivity and will likely already be included on board as part of a low order wavefront sensor for HWO. Here we present results from the HiCAT testbed at STScI where we calibrated gain maps for our Boston Micromachines 952-actuator micro electromechanical (MEMS) DMs using both a Fizeau interferometer and a ZWFS to compare performance. With the ZWFS we compute a gain map using both local linear fits around a given DM solution, and present a formalism for deriving more complex quadratic solutions which are more computationally intensive, but accurate over most of the dynamic range of each actuator. We then use these techniques to calibrate the DMs on the HiCAT testbed and show increased DZ digging efficiency with the new gain map and better contrast performance moving from 14 to 16 bit control electronics as enabled by these calibrations.

</details>


### [133] [Directional Anisotropic Sensitivity Curves for Pulsar Timing Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00250)
*Daniel J. Oliver,Jeffrey S. Hazboun,Martine Maggi,Jeremy G. Baier,Kyle E. Gourlie*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息的各向异性引力波背景方向灵敏度分析框架，并在IPTA类脉冲星计时阵列上进行了验证与应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于超大质量黑洞双星有限数量导致随机引力波背景必然存在各向异性，需发展能刻画天空方向灵敏度差异的分析方法。

Method: 基于各向异性背景的Fisher形式主义，将方向Fisher信息重构成天空上的有效灵敏度曲线，区分辐射计与全Fisher估计器，并引入天空加权曲线；采用五种天空分解基（像素、球谐函数、平方根球谐函数、辐射计、主图）实现建模与分析。

Result: 在IPTA类阵列上成功表征角响应、分析Fisher结构、恢复注入的各向异性热点，并预测未来阵列灵敏度；相关框架已作为扩展集成至灵敏度软件hasasia。

Conclusion: 该框架为探测和刻画引力波背景各向异性提供了系统化、可推广的工具，显著提升了脉冲星计时阵列对定向源的分辨与定位能力。

Abstract: After two decades of observations, pulsar timing array collaborations have reported strong evidence for a stochastic gravitational wave background, most likely sourced by an inspiraling population of supermassive black hole binaries. Because that population is finite, anisotropy in the background is inevitable, producing hotspots on the sky that track the loudest binaries. Building on the Fisher formalism for anisotropic backgrounds, we recast the directional Fisher information as effective sensitivity curves on the sky, distinguishing the radiometer and full-Fisher estimators, along with a sky-weighted curve that reduces the anisotropic sky to a single spectrum and recovers the standard isotropic curve in the isotropic limit. We implement five sky-decomposition bases: pixel, spherical harmonic, square-root spherical harmonic, radiometer, and principal-map. We demonstrate the framework on an IPTA-like pulsar timing array: characterizing the angular response, analyzing the Fisher structure, recovering injected anisotropic hotspots, and forecasting future-array sensitivity. The framework is released as an extension to the sensitivity software package hasasia.

</details>


### [134] [A Focal-plane X-ray Polarimeter with Spectral and Timing Capabilities for Future Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00273)
*Hemanth Manikantan,Carlo Lefevre,Lorenzo Petrucci,Paolo Soffitta,Fabio Muleri,Enrico Costa,Alda Rubini,Vladislavs Plesanovs,Markus Gruber,Jochen Kaminski,Klaus Desch,Alessandro Di Marco,Sergio Fabiani,Riccardo Ferrazzoli,Saba Imtiaz,Dawoon E. Kim,Alessandro Lacerenza,John Rankin,Ajay Ratheesh*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型X射线偏振探测器原型，采用Ar/DME气体吸收X射线、Timepix3 ASIC与InGrid倍增结构实现单电子探测和三维光电子轨迹重建，覆盖2–30 keV能段，提升低能偏振灵敏度并支持高通量望远镜焦面应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IXPE任务虽确立了X射线偏振测量的重要性，但其Gas Pixel Detector在下一代成像偏振仪中需显著改进，尤其在能段扩展（至30 keV）和低能偏振灵敏度方面。

Method: 采用Ar/DME混合气体作为X射线吸收介质，结合Timepix3像素化CMOS读出ASIC与InGrid倍增结构，实现单初级电子探测；通过三维重建光电子轨迹提升偏振测量精度，并利用Timepix3无死区时间特性适配高通量X射线镜。

Result: 成功研制出覆盖2–30 keV的新型偏振探测器原型，在低中能段展现出优异的时间分辨能力和中等能量分辨能力。

Conclusion: 该原型为下一代高灵敏度、宽能段、焦面集成型X射线成像偏振仪提供了可行技术路径，有望推动高能天体物理偏振观测的发展。

Abstract: The NASA-ASI mission Imaging X-ray Polarimeter Explorer (IXPE) firmly established X-ray polarimetry as a core observation pillar of high-energy astrophysics, alongside imaging, timing, and spectroscopy. While IXPE made groundbreaking discoveries in the 2$-$8 keV range on both point and extended X-ray sources, the Gas Pixel Detectors onboard IXPE demand substantial improvements to be ready for the next generation of imaging X-ray polarimeters. Here, we present the development of a detector prototype that can deliver the next generation of sensitive imaging X-ray polarimetry across the energy band of 2--30 keV. We use Ar/DME based gas volume to absorb X-rays by the photoelectric effect and a pixelated CMOS readout ASIC, Timepix3, with excellent timing capabilities to image the resulting photoelectron track in three dimensions. The Timepix3 is integrated with a multiplication stage called InGrid that enables single-primary-electron detection from the gas volume. The photoelectron track reconstructed in three dimensions increases the polarimetric sensitivity towards the lowest operable energies, and the deadtime-free operation of the ASIC facilitates the usage as a focal plane instrument on high-throughput X-ray mirrors. With this prototype, we demonstrate a low-medium-energy X-ray polarimeter with excellent timing and moderate spectral capabilities.

</details>


### [135] [A Comparative Systems-Engineering Framework for RFI Coexistence in Radio Astronomy and Aviation Safety Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00275)
*Simthembile Dlamini*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的共存裕度框架M(f,d)，将射电天文与航空安全系统的干扰保护标准统一起来，并通过两个案例研究验证其有效性，发现两者在设计上收敛于相同的三杠杆解决方案，并指出射电干涉测量技术可直接复用于航空系统相关任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射电天文与航空安全系统虽处于信号功率谱两端，却共享有限频谱资源，且均受到商用宽带无线服务日益加剧的挤压；二者目前采用相互独立、看似不可比的接收机保护标准，亟需统一框架以协调频谱共存问题。

Method: 提出共存裕度框架M(f,d) = Pth(f) - Prx(f,d)，将其应用于南非Karoo射电宁静区及全球5G C波段/无线电高度计争议两个案例，并分析射电干涉测量中的干扰消除、波束成形和统计校准等技术与航空系统（如ADS-B验证、相控阵雷达杂波抑制、预测性维护异常检测）之间的映射关系。

Result: 证实ITUR RA.769与RTCA SC-239标准均为该框架的特例；两个案例均收敛于‘保护带隔离、受限排除区、接收端滤波’三杠杆方案，并依赖强制协调而非静态排除；射电天文多项数据处理技术可直接复用于航空系统关键任务。

Conclusion: M(f,d)是首个跨射电天文与航空安全两大领域的通用形式化共存框架；其揭示的共性设计模式及工具链映射，为窄带接收系统与快速增长的宽带商用系统间的频谱共存提供了可复用的设计范式、技能共享路径与基础设施协同基础。

Abstract: Radio astronomy and aviation safety systems occupy opposite ends of the signal-power spectrum, yet both depend on the same finite resource and are increasingly squeezed by commercial broadband wireless services. The two fields protect their receivers using separately derived, apparently incommensurable criteria: the ITU-R RA.769 detrimental-interference threshold for radio telescopes, and the radar-altimeter interference thresholds established by RTCA Special Committee 239 for the 5G C-band coexistence problem. We introduce a single coexistence-margin framework, M(f,d) = Pth(f) - Prx(f,d), and show both criteria to be special cases of it. We apply this framework to two case studies, the Karoo Radio Quiet Zone surrounding MeerKAT and SKA-Mid, and the global 5G C-band/altimeter dispute, showing that, despite unrelated regulatory histories, both converged on the same three-lever solution: guard-band separation, bounded exclusion zones, and receiver-side filtering, governed by mandatory coordination rather than static exclusion. We then show that interference-excision, beamforming, and statistical-calibration pipelines developed for radio interferometry are closely analogous to, and in several cases directly reusable for, operations required by ADS-B validation, phased-array radar clutter rejection, and predictive-maintenance anomaly detection. The unifying framework, and the finding that two independently-arising regulatory histories converge on the same design pattern, is this papers central contribution: M(f,d) has not, to our knowledge, been proposed before as a common formalism spanning these two protection regimes, and the toolkit mapping distinguishes genuine reuse from looser analogy. The result is a reusable design pattern and shared skills/infrastructure pipeline, applicable wherever a narrowband receiver must coexist with a growing broadband commercial neighbour.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BICEP Array（BA）实验中用于测量宇宙微波背景（CMB）偏振的带通滤波器（BPF）设计与性能测试，重点在于简化电路结构（无并联电感）、使用Sonnet仿真优化，并在南极及实验室开展220 GHz和270 GHz探测器模块的频率响应实测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确约束BICEP Array接收机的频率响应，需设计高性能、易制造的带通滤波器，以支持CMB B模偏振的高灵敏度测量。

Method: 采用仅含串联/并联电容和串联电感（不含并联电感）的简化电路结构；利用Sonnet软件进行设计与仿真；结合大气和CMB负载进行噪声优化；在南极现场测试220 GHz模块，在实验室测试270 GHz模块。

Result: 多个220 GHz探测器模块已在南极完成频率响应实测；270 GHz模块已完成实验室初步测试，其在BA接收机中的性能将于今年冬季实测。

Conclusion: 所设计的简化BPF结构兼顾性能与可制造性，实测结果验证了其在实际观测环境下的可行性，为BA后续高精度CMB偏振测量奠定关键硬件基础。

Abstract: The BICEP Array (BA) is the latest in the BICEP/ Keck series of experiments that aim to measure the polarization of the cosmic microwave background (CMB) with small aperture polarimeters located at the South Pole. To constrain the frequency response of these receivers, each detector is serially coupled to a band-pass filter (BPF). The electric circuits of these BPFs utilize series and shunt capacitors as well as series inductors, but critically do not include shunt inductors which simplifies fabrication. The filters are designed and simulated with Sonnet, and optimized for noise by considering loading from the atmosphere and the CMB. Multiple 220 GHz detector modules have had their frequency response measured at the South Pole. The 270 GHz detector modules have recently begun testing in a lab setting, and their performance in a BA receiver will be measured this winter.

</details>


### [137] [Investigating the differential limb coupling effect for diffraction-limited spectrographs with PARVI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00349)

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文首次直接测量了差分临边耦合（DLC）效应对高精度视向速度（RV）的影响，使用衍射极限光谱仪PARVI验证理论预测，并为未来仪器HISPEC等开发缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分临边耦合（DLC）在单模光纤+自适应光学的EPRV光谱仪中会引入m/s量级的RV误差，亟需实测验证与校正。

Method: 设计可产生数百m/s DLC-RV偏移的观测策略，在衍射极限光谱仪PARVI上开展实验，并表征其Tip-Tilt导星相机性能。

Result: 首次获得DLC引起的RV偏移实测数据，但RV信号与仪器及算法效应深度耦合，分析复杂。

Conclusion: DLC效应真实存在且显著，需在HISPEC等下一代EPRV仪器中系统建模并加以抑制。

Abstract: A promising new architecture for extreme-precision radial velocity (EPRV) spectrographs, hunting for small-amplitude stellar Doppler shifts induced by orbiting planets, is to build diffraction-limited instruments by using single-mode fibers fed by an adaptive optics system. However, the target stars are partially resolved when observing at the diffraction limit, and the resulting RVs are expected to be affected by differential limb coupling (DLC), an effect where the red- and blue-shifted sides of the stellar disk are coupled unequally into the spectrograph, producing an RV error term on the order of m/s for nearby EPRV target stars for the upcoming HISPEC spectrograph for Keck II. We present our efforts to directly measure the RV shifts resulting from DLC for the first time, using the diffraction-limited spectrograph PARVI, in order to verify the expected behavior of DLC and subsequently develop mitigation strategies for HISPEC and other future instruments. We outline an observing strategy designed to produce DLC-induced RV shifts of hundreds of m/s, and describe the execution of this experiment with PARVI. We use these data to characterize the PARVI tip-tilt guide camera and its performance, and have begun analysis of the derived RVs, though this investigation has proven complicated since the RVs are deeply entangled with other instrumental and algorithmic effects.

</details>


### [138] [Fundamental Noise Limits of Infrared Detectors in the Presence of Readout Glow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00354)
*Michael Bottom,Gustav Pettersson,Pavaman Bilgi,Guillaume Huber,Charles-Antoine Claveau,Nathan Lourie,Robert Simcoe,Shane Jacobson*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文揭示了红外传感器阵列中读出噪声降低受限于读出辉光（readout glow）这一新机制，并提出了相应的理论噪声下限，通过实验验证了该模型在HxRG和LmAPD传感器上的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极大型望远镜和宜居世界天文台（HWO）等下一代天文设备受限于红外探测器的读出噪声，亟需突破现有降噪策略的瓶颈。

Method: 提出并建模读出辉光导致的非理想平均效应，推导其引起的噪声下限公式σ_min ~ 1.5 σ_RN^(1/2) G^(1/4)，并通过HxRG与线性模式雪崩光电二极管（LmAPD）的实验室数据验证该模型。

Result: 实验表明LmAPD的读出噪声在宽范围平均次数下与理论预测吻合（误差<0.1 e⁻），HxRG类传感器受辉光限制噪声下限约2–3 e⁻，LmAPD约为0.5 e⁻。

Conclusion: 读出辉光是制约多非破坏性读取降噪效果的关键物理因素，所提出的噪声下限模型为未来低噪声红外探测器设计与优化提供了理论基础和实验依据。

Abstract: Read noise in infrared sensor arrays remains a major obstacle for ground- and space-based astronomy. It has long been recognized that the upcoming extremely large telescopes cannot meet their full potential unless read noise is significantly improved, and it is also a prohibitive constraint on the Habitable Worlds Observatory, a space telescope with the goal of detection and characterization of nearby Earth-like exoplanets.
  The main strategy for lowering read noise is averaging through multiple non-destructive reads. However, this typically results in less noise reduction than the 1/$\sqrt{N}$ scaling predicted by theory. In this work, we show the poor averaging behavior can largely be explained by readout glow, photon emission from the sensor electronics that generates photoelectrons in the pixels during readout. Because glow accumulates with reads rather than averaging, this imposes a fundamental noise floor of σ_{\rm min} ~ 1.5 sigma_RN^(1/2)G^(1/4). This limits averaging in HxRG-like sensors to about 2-3 e- of noise, and linear-mode avalanche photodiodes (LmAPDs) to about 0.5 e-. We present laboratory data using both sensor architectures, with the LmAPD following the predicted noise value to within 0.1 e- over two decades of averaging.

</details>


### [139] [Facility integration of the NASA IRTF adaptive secondary mirror](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00373)
*Ellen Lee,Mark Chun,Michael Connelley,Ruihan Zhang,Olivier Lai,Tony Denault,John Rayner,Max Baeten,Arjo Bos,Matias Kidron,Fred Kamphues,Stefan Kuiper,Wouter Jonker,Alan Ryan,Philip Hinz*

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IRTF-ASM-1自适应副镜自2024年首次运行以来的性能表现，并阐述了其在主动光学模式下与Felix波前传感器协同优化望远镜像质的软件开发进展，以及消除非共光路低阶像差的技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用IRTF-ASM-1的物理鲁棒性和已验证的灵敏度提升能力，在无AO专家支持的IRTF上实现每晚自动优化图像质量，以提升科学观测效果。

Method: 基于单共轭Felix 2x2夏克-哈特曼波前传感器，开发适用于IRTF-ASM-1的主动光学控制软件；并提出校正Felix与科学仪器之间低阶、大振幅非共光路像差的技术方法。

Result: 成功推进了IRTF-ASM-1在主动光学模式下的软件开发，并实现了对关键非共光路像差的有效校正。

Conclusion: IRTF-ASM-1不仅验证了新型混合可变磁阻执行器技术，还为缺乏自适应光学专业能力的中小型望远镜提供了实用、低干预的实时像质优化方案。

Abstract: IRTF-ASM-1 has been functioning well since its first light in 2024. This adaptive secondary mirror (ASM) was primarily developed to be an on-sky demonstration of the new hybrid variable reluctance actuator technology at the NASA Infrared Telescope Facility (IRTF). However, due to its physical robustness and our previous demonstrations of sensitivity enhancements with the ASM, we are interested in using it to optimize telescope image quality on a nightly basis. This will directly benefit science observations. However, as IRTF does not currently have adaptive optics expertise, we have been developing the system to be used with minimal human intervention. We present our progress in developing software for active optics mode with IRTF-ASM-1 using the single conjugate, facility 2x2 Shack-Hartmann wavefront sensor Felix. We also present techniques for removing low-order, large amplitude non-common path aberrations between Felix and our science instruments.

</details>


### [140] [Development and Evaluation of a CNN-Based Charged-Particle Event Rejection Algorithm for Soft X-ray Detection in a pnCCD-Based Satellite System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00476)
*Hsien-Chieh Shen,Ryuji Kondo,Makoto Arimoto,Tatsuro Kanenaga,Hiro Otsuka,Shutaro Ueda,Junko Hiraga,Daisuke Yonetoku,Tatsuya Sawano,Takanori Sakamoto,Hiroshi Tomida,Akihiro Doi,Hiroshi Nakajima,Takaaki Tanaka,Robert Hartmann,Lothar Strüder*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于卷积神经网络（CNN）的pnCCD探测器事件分类方法，显著降低了空间带电粒子背景误判率（从10.2–11.9%降至3.1%），同时保持高X射线事件接受率，适用于HiZ-GUNDAM卫星等未来宽视场软X射线巡天任务的星上实时筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HiZ-GUNDAM卫星任务受限于遥测带宽，需在星上进行事件筛选；但空间带电粒子易被误判为X射线光子，导致背景污染、灵敏度下降和假警报。

Method: 开发了pnCCD读出系统，利用Fe-55（X射线）和Sr-90（电子）源标定数据，对比传统grade法与CNN对事件分类的性能；并结合薄耗尽层CMOS传感器进行性能参照分析。

Result: CNN将带电粒子误分类率从10.2–11.9%降至3.1%，尤其在高沉积能量区效果更优；厚耗尽层pnCCD比薄耗尽CMOS具有更优的粒子鉴别能力。

Conclusion: CNN方法可在保证高X射线接受率前提下显著抑制带电粒子污染，是未来pnCCD宽视场X射线巡天任务星上实时事件筛选的可行且高效方案。

Abstract: All-sky surveys in the soft X-ray band are essential for detecting transient objects such as high-redshift gamma-ray bursts (GRBs), which provide key insights into the early universe. HiZ-GUNDAM is a future satellite mission designed to detect and localize high-redshift GRBs. Its wide-field X-ray monitor, EAGLE, combines Lobster Eye Optics with a pnCCD imaging detector operating in the 0.4-4 keV band. Because of limited satellite telemetry, full-frame pnCCD images cannot be downlinked, requiring onboard event selection. Charged particles in the space environment produce background events that can be misidentified as X-ray photons, degrading detection sensitivity and potentially triggering false alerts. In this study, we developed a pnCCD readout system and evaluated charged-particle rejection using conventional grade methods and a convolutional neural network (CNN). Performance was evaluated using X-ray events from an Fe-55 source and electron events from a Sr-90 beta source. The CNN reduced the misclassification rate from 10.2-11.9% for conventional grade methods to 3.1% while maintaining a high acceptance rate for X-ray events. The improvement is particularly pronounced at higher deposited energies, reflecting the CNN's ability to distinguish track-like particle events from X-ray events by capturing detailed spatial features of charge distributions. Comparison with a thin-depletion-layer CMOS sensor further indicates that the thicker depletion layer of the pnCCD enhances discrimination performance. These results demonstrate that CNN-based event classification can substantially reduce charged-particle contamination while maintaining high X-ray acceptance, making it a promising approach for onboard event selection in future pnCCD-based wide-field X-ray missions.

</details>


### [141] [Pollux: high-resolution precision spectroscopy and polarimetry for the Habitable Worlds Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00522)
*David Le Mignant,Coralie Neiner,Jean-Claude Bouret,Luca Fossati,Eduard Muslimov,Kjetil Dohlen,Anne Bonnefoi,Jean-Michel Reess,Mélissa Alliguié,Sreejith A. G.,Aquib Moine,Adrien Girardot,for the Pollux consortium*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: Pollux is a proposed high-resolution spectrograph and spectropolarimeter for NASA's Habitable Worlds Observatory, designed to advance studies of stars, exoplanets, and cosmic ecosystems through broad spectral coverage, high resolution, stability, and novel UV polarimetry.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To revolutionize the study of stellar and (exo)planetary systems and cosmic ecosystems by enabling observations in a new astrophysical parameter space.

Method: Design and proposal of Pollux, a high-resolution (R = 65000–100000), broad-coverage (100–1750 nm), temporally stable spectrograph and spectropolarimeter with unique UV spectropolarimetric capabilities.

Result: Pollux would provide unprecedented simultaneous broad-band, high-resolution, and UV polarimetric data, opening new observational avenues.

Conclusion: Pollux represents a transformative instrument concept for HWO, uniquely positioned to address key questions in astrophysics and astrobiology.

Abstract: Pollux is a high-resolution spectrograph and spectropolarimeter (R from 65000 to 100000) covering a spectral range from 100 nm to 1750 nm, proposed by a European consortium to equip NASA s Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO). This instrument aims to revolutionize the study of stellar and (exo)planetary systems, as well as cosmic ecosystems, by combining high spectral resolution, broad and simultaneous spectral coverage, temporal stability, and unique UV spectropolarimetric capabilities, thus opening a new parameter space for astrophysics.

</details>


### [142] [Conceptual architecture of the detector infrastructure for WST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00734)
*A. Meoli,O. Iwert,E. George,O. Squalli,M. Richerzhagen,A. Rüde,M. Seidel,S. Lévêque*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于大型宽视场光谱望远镜（如WST）的分布式探测器控制器架构，聚焦于系统级设计、供电分配与数据处理，并探讨其随项目发展的时间演化路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WST需 unprecedented 数量的探测器，带来系统级控制、供电与数据处理的巨大挑战。

Method: 提出一种基于靠近探测器部署的模块化单元和网络化后端电子设备的分布式探测器控制器架构。

Result: 给出了适用于WST等大型设施的探测器控制器概念架构，涵盖系统级设计、供电分布与数据处理方案。

Conclusion: 该分布式架构可扩展性强，具备随项目发展演进的能力，为未来超大规模天文探测器控制系统提供了可行技术路径。

Abstract: The Wide-field Spectroscopic Telescope (WST) is a proposed 12 m wide-field spectroscopic facility combining several multi-object spectrographs. It requires a yet unprecedented number of detectors. In this paper we present conceptual architecture for the detector controller and infrastructure required to operate a large number of detectors, potentially applicable to WST, focusing on the system-level, power distribution, and the associated data handling. We also consider how these elements may evolve over the expected development timeline of such a facility. Motivated by the scale of the problem, we outline a possible distributed detector-controller architecture, based on modular units placed close to the detectors and networked backend electronics.

</details>


### [143] [webSME: An online tool to infer stellar parameters and abundances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00787)
*Johannes Puschnig,Andreas J. Korn,Jonathan Remmert,Ivy Balkwill-Western,Chi Than Nguyen,Nikolai Piskunov*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了webSME——一个基于Web的SME（Spectroscopy Made Easy）Python实现扩展平台，集成了非局部热动平衡（NLTE）丰度修正、预计算合成光谱网格、MCMC不确定性估计及最新参考丰度模式等功能，提升了高分辨率恒星光谱参数测定的效率与精度，并通过合成与实测光谱验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升恒星光谱分析的易用性、效率和精度，支持大规模光谱数据处理及教育科普等多样化应用需求。

Method: 开发基于Web的SME Python实现扩展平台webSME，集成NLTE丰度修正、预计算合成光谱网格、MCMC采样及现代丰度参考模式。

Result: webSME在合成与实测光谱（含标准星）上表现出良好性能，结果与经典SME分析一致，具备高精度参数测定能力。

Conclusion: webSME是一个功能先进、易于访问的现代化恒星光谱分析工具，显著拓展了SME的应用范围与实用性。

Abstract: Stellar spectroscopy is a robust technique for determining fundamental stellar parameters such as effective temperature, surface gravity and metallicity. Spectroscopy Made Easy (SME) has long served as a framework for spectral synthesis and parameter inference. In this paper, we introduce webSME, a web-based extension of the Python implementation of SME. webSME integrates enhancements including a non-local thermodynamic equilibrium abundance correction mode, a precomputed grid of synthetic spectra for robust determination of stellar parameters from large wavelength ranges (thousands of Angstroms wide), Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling for uncertainty estimation, and support for recent reference abundance patterns. It enables efficient and user-friendly analysis of high-resolution spectra, making it suitable for a wide range of applications - from detailed abundance studies to education and outreach. We demonstrate the performance of webSME on synthetic and observed spectra, including benchmark stars, and validate its accuracy against classical SME-based analysis. The platform's ease of access and advanced capabilities position it as a powerful tool in the modern astrophysical toolkit.

</details>


### [144] [Qualification pathways for Photonic Integrated Circuits in Astrophotonic Space Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00834)
*Kalaga Madhav,Aashia Rahman,Máté Ádámkovics*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对天基光子集成电路（PICs）的空间认证框架，整合NASA和ESA现有标准，构建了七阶段PIC-SQT认证模板，并分析了不同材料平台的辐射耐受性及光纤飞行历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏专门针对光子集成电路（PICs）的空间认证标准，而PICs在天文、行星和地球观测任务中具有显著减小尺寸、重量和功耗（SWaP）的潜力。

Method: 系统梳理NASA与ESA相关认证文档，构建包含19项测试步骤的主认证表；结合两种典型任务剖面（LEO小卫星与HWO级L2旗舰任务）建立定制化矩阵；绘制TRL与测试覆盖映射图；调研UV/Vis/NIR平台特性与辐射效应；总结40余年光纤飞行经验。

Result: 提出了七阶段PIC专用空间认证模板（PIC-SQT），明确了TRL关口、具体测试流程与任务级别定制方法；识别出当前标准未涵盖但PIC必需的认证环节；确认二氧化硅、Si3N4及激光直写波导芯具有本征抗辐射能力，而III-V族与Ge有源器件需承担主要抗辐照设计负担。

Conclusion: 该工作为天基光子集成电路提供了首个系统化、可裁剪、面向任务的空间认证路径，填补了标准空白，支撑其向高TRL迈进并实际应用于未来空间光学载荷。

Abstract: Photonic integrated circuits (PICs) promise order-of-magnitude reductions in the size, weight and power (SWaP) of optical subsystems for astronomy, planetary and Earth-observation missions, yet no PIC-specific space-qualification standard exists. This paper consolidates the principal NASA and ESA qualification documents that apply, or can be tailored, to astrophotonic PICs --- arrayed waveguide gratings, photonic lanterns, fibre Bragg gratings, and integrated beam combiners (ABCD, discrete beam combiners, nullers) for spectrographs and stellar interferometers. A master qualification table lists 19 standard test steps with applicable standards and EU/USA test facilities. Two reference mission profiles --- a LEO smallsat demonstrator and an HWO-class Lagrange-2 flagship --- yield a tailoring matrix, while a TRL-versus-test-coverage roadmap maps each activity onto the NASA/ESA readiness levels and review gates. A survey of UV/visible/near-infrared platforms relates spectral coverage, maturity and flight heritage, and a radiation-effects summary shows passive silica, Si3N4 and laser-written cores are essentially radiation-tolerant while active III--V and Ge devices carry the hardness burden. The central outcome is a seven-phase qualification template (PIC-SQT) with explicit TRL gates, exact test procedures and mission-class tailoring; we further identify qualification processes relevant to PICs that current standards do not cover, and document 40+ years of optical-fibre flight heritage.

</details>


### [145] [Black Hole Explorer Mission Development in Japan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00890)
*Kazunori Akiyama,Mareki Honma,Akihiro Doi,Shoko Koyama,Kotaro Niinuma,Kazuhiro Hada,Yuto Akiyama,Rebecca Baturin,Peter Galison,Paul Grimes,Yoshiaki Hagiwara,Takayuki J. Hayashi,Aya E. Higuchi,Janice Houston,Michael D. Johnson,Tomohisa Kawashima,Daniel P. Marrone,Yosuke Murayama,Ken Ohsuga,Hannah Rana,Hidetoshi Sano,Edward Tong,Yuh Tsunetoe,Yoshinori Uzawa*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了日本在Black Hole Explorer (BHEX)空间VLBI任务中的进展，包括成立工作小组、关键仪器技术研发（如4.5K闭循环机械制冷机和300 GHz SIS混频器原型）及地面观测设施升级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将地面毫米/亚毫米波VLBI阵列拓展至太空，提升黑洞成像等高分辨率观测能力。

Method: 通过成立BHEX日本联盟与ISAS的BHEX工作组开展预研；开展低温制冷机与超宽带SIS混频器的设计与原型研制；升级VERA和野边山45米望远镜以支持多频段协同观测。

Result: 已建立BHEX WG并推进预研；完成4.5K制冷机概念设计及300 GHz SIS混频器原型开发；实现VERA在86 GHz观测能力升级，并推动86+230 GHz双频同步观测。

Conclusion: 日本正稳步推进BHEX任务关键技术攻关与地面支持能力建设，为未来空间VLBI任务奠定基础。

Abstract: The Black Hole Explorer (BHEX) is a next-generation space very-long-baseline interferometry (VLBI) mission concept that will extend existing ground-based millimeter/submillimeter VLBI arrays to space. The Japanese astronomical community has contributed to BHEX mission development through the BHEX Japan Consortium, established in 2023. This paper provides a high-level summary of progress in Japan since 2024, including the establishment of the Black Hole Explorer Working Group (BHEX WG) at the Institute of Space and Astronautical Science (ISAS), JAXA, to conduct the Japanese side of the Pre-Phase~A mission studies. We outline recent advances in key instrument technologies, including concept design studies of a 4.5\,K closed-cycle mechanical cryocooler and prototype development of an ultra-wideband 300\,GHz Superconductor--Insulator--Superconductor (SIS) mixer for BHEX. We also describe ongoing upgrades to Japan's ground infrastructure to support 86\,GHz observations with VERA and simultaneous 86+230\,GHz observations with the Nobeyama 45\,m Telescope.

</details>


### [146] [Towards optimal photometric calibration of digital astronomical plates with deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01391)
*Mingyang Ma,Haibo Yuan,Lin Yang,Kai Xiao,Bowen Huang,Shiyin Shen,Zhengjun Shang,Yong Yu,Meiting Yang,Zhenghong Tang,Jianhai Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MFF-Net的深度学习光度定标框架，用于解决历史照相底片数字化过程中因图像质量空间变化与星等耦合导致的传统可分离模型失效问题；该方法联合建模星等、颜色与位置信息，在1200张中国历史底片上显著优于MYX25方法，尤其对亮星精度提升约两倍，并有效消除残差中的星等–位置耦合系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光度定标模型假设星等、颜色和位置效应可分离，但在图像质量随视场空间变化且与星等耦合时失效，导致残差中存在耦合的空间系统误差。

Method: 提出多特征融合网络（MFF-Net），以仪器星等、颜色和像素坐标为输入，端到端学习一个非线性联合校正函数，直接建模三者间的耦合依赖关系。

Result: 在1200张中国数字化照相底片上测试表明，MFF-Net将5–95百分位精度从0.11–0.26 mag提升至0.08–0.18 mag，对亮星精度提升约两倍，并显著削弱残差图中的星等–位置耦合结构。

Conclusion: MFF-Net能更准确地建模历史底片中复杂的耦合系统误差，提升定标精度，增强大规模历史底片档案的科学可用性。

Abstract: Photometric calibration of digitized photographic plates is commonly modeled with separable magnitude-, color-, and position-dependent terms, but this separability can break down when image quality varies across the field in a magnitude-dependent way, leaving coupled spatial systematics in the residuals. We introduce a deep-learning calibration framework, the Multi-Feature Fused Network (MFF-Net), which takes instrumental magnitude, color, and pixel coordinates as input and learns a single nonlinear correction that jointly captures their coupled dependencies. Tests on 1{,}200 digitized Chinese plates show that MFF-Net consistently outperforms the MYX25 method (Ma et al. 2025), improving the 5th--95th percentile precision from 0.11--0.26~mag to 0.08--0.18~mag and delivering an approximately factor-of-two gain for bright sources. The learned correction largely removes the magnitude--position coupling seen in post-calibration residual maps, enabling higher-precision plate photometry and more reliable use of large historical plate archives.

</details>


### [147] [Design and modeling of an oblique incidence dichroic mirror for a high contrast imaging system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01394)
*Joanna Rosenbluth,Ramya Anche,Kyle Van Gorkom,Nikoli Cooper,William Melby,Daewook Kim,Ewan Douglas*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种用于宜居世界天文台（HWO）的长波通二向色镜，通过多层薄膜优化，在550 nm附近实现锐截止、低偏振像差和低色散波前误差，仿真表明其引起的暗区对比度残差达10⁻¹²量级，不影响日冕仪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HWO需在紫外至红外波段对类地行星进行高对比度成像，而二向色镜涂层若引入显著偏振像差和色散波前误差，将限制成像对比度。

Method: 采用多层薄膜设计方法，优化反射相位以降低制造厚度误差导致的波前误差，并结合矢量涡旋日冕仪仿真评估对比度性能。

Result: 设计出在7°入射角下工作、550 nm附近锐截止的长波通二向色镜，涂层引起的波前误差经传播后，暗区对比度残差达10⁻¹²量级。

Conclusion: 该优化二向色镜不会成为HWO日冕仪性能的瓶颈，满足高对比度系外行星成像需求。

Abstract: The proposed Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO) aims to detect and characterize Earth-like planets around Sun-like stars from the ultraviolet to the infrared using high-contrast imaging. A dichroic mirror is a natural choice for splitting light between multiple wavelength channels in such a system, but the coating must introduce minimal polarization aberrations and chromatic wavefront errors (WFE) to avoid limiting contrast. We present the design of a long-pass dichroic mirror operated at a $7^\circ$ angle of incidence that provides high reflection in the short-wavelength band, high transmission in the long-wavelength band, and a sharp cut-on near 550~nm. A multilayer thin-film design process was used to meet these spectral requirements while reducing polarization aberrations and chromatic WFE sensitivity. Reflected phase optimization was also incorporated to reduce sensitivity to manufacturing-induced layer-thickness variations, which can produce wavelength-dependent WFE and degrade coronagraph performance. The resulting coating-induced WFE was propagated through simulations of a vector vortex coronagraph to assess contrast performance. Simulations predict dark-hole contrast residuals at the $10^{-12}$ level, indicating that the optimized dichroic mirror does not limit coronagraph performance.

</details>


### [148] [MiraSOL: a DMD-based spectrograph for resolved solar spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01493)
*Christian Robles,Suvrath Mahadevan,Lawrence Ramsey*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: MiraSOL是一个基于数字微镜技术（DLP）的新太阳观测实验平台，旨在实现太阳盘面上特定区域的光谱选择性观测，用于研究恒星活动引起的径向速度信号及恒星污染对系外行星透射光谱的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决恒星活动（如黑子、谱斑）引起的径向速度噪声问题，并量化其对系外行星透射光谱的污染效应，以提升系外行星大气表征精度。

Method: 采用TI DLP801RE数字微镜器件作为空间光调制器，实现3角秒分辨率的太阳盘面掩模；结合高/低分辨率光谱仪（如HPF、NEID）进行径向速度与透射光谱测量；开展实验室测试与原理样机验证。

Result: 成功构建原理样机，实现全太阳盘面成像与像素级控制；发现并识别出60 Hz闪烁信号源于商用评估板电子系统；验证了该技术在太阳观测中的可行性。

Conclusion: MiraSOL为研究恒星活动对系外行星探测的影响提供了新工具，其基于DLP的空间选择性观测能力具备实用前景，但需进一步优化电子噪声问题。

Abstract: We present the science motivation, preliminary design requirements, device laboratory testing and a prototype for an new experimental platform for solar observations, MiraSOL. MiraSOL will use digital micromirror technology to actively select regions on the solar disk for spectroscopic observation to determine the spatially dependent radial velocity signatures of stellar variability, and can create transits on the solar disk to probe the effects of stellar contamination on exoplanet transmission spectra. MiraSOL uses the Texas Instruments DLP801RE as a spatial light modulator to allow a mask, with 3 arcsecond spatial sampling per micromirror, capable of resolving features on the solar disk. This instrument will have a fiber output which can then be coupled with state-of-the-art extreme precision radial velocity (EPRV) spectrometers, such as HPF or NEID, for high resolving power, stable spectra of sunspots and plage, or a low resolution spectrometer for studies of stellar contamination in transit spectra. We discuss a 60 Hz flicker signal we discovered, likely due to the commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) evaluation board of the digital micromirror device electronics. We also build a proof-of-concept prototype and demonstrate imaging and pixel-level control of the full solar disk to demonstrate the feasibility of this technology.

</details>


### [149] [In orbit background for hard X-ray CubeSat polarimeters: case study of the CUSP mission in low-earth orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01524)
*Abhay Kumar,Giovanni Lombardi,Riccardo Campana,Giovanni De Cesare,Sergio Fabiani,Ettore Del Monte,Andrea Alimenti,Enrico Costa,Mauro Centrone,Nicolas De Angelis,Sergio Di Cosimo,Giuseppe Di Persio,Pasqualino Loffredo,Gabriele Minervini,Fabio Muleri,Paolo Romano,Alda Rubini,Emanuele Scalise,Enrico Silva,Paolo Soffitta,Giovanni Cucinella,Vito Di Bari,Simone Di Filippo,Andrea Negri,Massimo Perelli,Dario Modenini,Andrea Curatolo,Nicolas Gagliardi,Daniele Pecorella,Alice Ponti,Paolo Tortora,Andrea Del Re,Davide Albanesi,Valerio Campamaggiore,Giulia de Iulis,Adam Ebrahim,Paolo Leonetti,Marco Schepis,Alessandro Zambardi,Ilaria Baffo,Pierluigi Fanelli,Costantino Zazza,Valerio Vagelli,Daniele Brienza,Immacolata Donnarumma,Matteo Merge,Emanuele Zaccagnino,Alessandro Turchi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: CUSP项目旨在利用康普顿散射偏振计测量太阳耀斑在25-100 keV硬X射线波段的线偏振，本文基于Geant4模拟器初步估计了其在轨背景辐射，以优化信噪比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为优化CUSP立方星偏振计在轨信号与背景比，需准确估计低地球轨道中宇宙射线、反照X射线、带电粒子及次级辐射等背景贡献。

Method: 采用Geant4蒙特卡洛仿真工具，构建CUSP卫星详细质量模型，模拟各类在轨背景辐射成分并估算背景计数率。

Result: 获得了CUSP任务在预定轨道上的初步在轨背景辐射估计结果，为探测器几何结构和屏蔽设计优化提供了依据。

Conclusion: Geant4仿真表明，通过合理建模与背景评估，可有效支持CUSP偏振计的性能优化，为其科学目标实现奠定基础。

Abstract: The CUbesat Solar Polarimeter (CUSP) project aims to measure the linear polarization of solar flares in the 25 - 100 keV hard X-ray band using a Compton scattering polarimeter. CUSP is a project in the framework of the Alcor Program of the Italian Space Agency aimed to develop innovative CubeSat technologies and missions. As part of CUSPs Phase B study, initiated in December 2024 and closed on July 2nd, 2026, estimating the in orbit background to optimize the signal-to-background ratio was one of the key objectives. In low-Earth orbit, the instrument is exposed to cosmic and albedo X-ray backgrounds, charged particles, and secondary radiation from the spacecraft and atmosphere. Simulating these contributions enables optimization of detector geometry and shielding to maximize signal-to-noise performance.
  We present initial in orbit background estimates for CUSP using a Geant4-based simulator. A detailed mass model of the CUSP has been implemented to simulate background components and estimate the background count rate in the CUSP orbit.

</details>


### [150] [Hard X-Ray Focal-Plane Compton Spectro-Polarimeter: Detector Development and Sensitivity Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01529)
*Abhay Kumar,Tanmoy Chattopadhyay,Santosh V. Vadawale,N. P. S. Mithun*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种基于康普顿散射的硬X射线谱偏振仪（CXPOL第二代），采用位置灵敏塑料散射器与环绕式位置灵敏吸收器阵列，结合Geant4模拟与原型探测器表征，验证其在20–80 keV波段的高偏振测量性能与光谱能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线偏振测量长期受限于技术挑战，尤其在硬X射线波段；随着硬X射线聚焦光学的发展，高灵敏度焦面康普顿偏振仪成为可能。

Method: 基于CXPOL第一代原型，设计新型位置灵敏康普顿谱偏振仪；利用Geant4模拟评估调制因子、偏振效率及光学耦合下的探测灵敏度；同时研制并表征100×20×5 mm³双端SiPM读出NaI(Tl)吸收器原型，测试能量/位置分辨率与光输出均匀性。

Result: 仿真表明新构型具备优良偏振性能；实验验证双端符合读出可将SiPM本底降低一个数量级，且NaI(Tl)探测器展现出良好能量与位置分辨能力。

Conclusion: 位置灵敏康普顿焦面仪器具备实现下一代硬X射线谱偏振观测的坚实技术基础与应用潜力。

Abstract: The scientific potential of X-ray polarimetry has long been recognized, yet the challenges of measuring polarization have left it largely unexplored, mainly in the hard X-ray regime. With the advent of hard X-ray focusing optics, sensitive focal-plane Compton polarimeters are now feasible. An early example is CXPOL (Compton X-ray Polarimeter), developed at Physical Research Laboratory (PRL), India, which demonstrated 20 - 80 keV polarimetric capabilities using a plastic scatterer and a CsI(Tl) absorber array. The CXPOL prototype demonstrated polarimetric capabilities in the 20 - 80 keV range, establishing a foundation for further development.
  Building on this concept, we evaluate a hard X-ray spectro-polarimeter employing a position-sensitive plastic scatterer surrounded by position-sensitive absorber detectors. This geometry enables efficient reconstruction of Compton events and allows combined polarimetric and spectroscopic measurements via interaction positions and deposited energies in the detectors. We evaluate key performance parameters of the revised configuration of the second version of the CXPOL. Using Geant4 simulations, we assess key performance parameters, including modulation factor, polarimetric efficiency, and expected sensitivity with modern hard X-ray optics. We also present the characterization results of first prototype of a 100x20x5 mm3 NaI(Tl) absorber read out on both ends by silicon photomultiplier (SiPM) array operating in coincidence, evaluating energy and position resolution and light-output variation along the detector. The coincidence readout also reduces SiPM background by an order of magnitude. The results demonstrate the strong potential of a position-sensitive Compton- based focal-plane instrument for next-generation hard X-ray spectro-polarimetry.

</details>


### [151] [Pollux UV & FUV polarimeters: first lab results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02118)
*Adrien Girardot,Ninon Demelier,Coralie Neiner,Jean-Michel Reess,Juan Ignacio Larruquert,Paloma Lopez-Reyes,Anejandro Garcia Canas,Nuria Gutierrez Luna,Ana Iglesias Moreno*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了为Habitable Worlds Observatory（HWO）设计的高分辨率光谱偏振仪Pollux的真空紫外测试平台及其初步实验室验证结果，验证了其在中近紫外（MUV-NUV）和远紫外（FUV）波段的偏振光学链路关键组件性能，为实现10⁻³级偏振精度奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持HWO任务，需发展具备高偏振精度（10⁻³）的紫外波段（97–1750 nm）光谱偏振测量能力；尤其在FUV波段缺乏可用双折射材料，需创新全反射式偏振器设计并验证其可行性。

Method: 构建专用真空紫外测试平台，分为MUV-NUV（120–290 nm）与FUV（98–120 nm）两套配置；通过氘灯偏振光谱采集、穆勒矩阵建模比对、K镜调制器对准、反射式分析器测试等手段开展实验室验证。

Result: MUV-NUV配置成功获取首条偏振光谱，验证了偏振产生子系统与理论预测一致，并完成首次端到端偏振测量；FUV配置完成等离子体光源表征、K镜对准实施及反射式分析器测试，在120 nm处实现10倍偏振消光比。

Conclusion: 测试平台已成功运行，关键偏振组件达到初步性能指标，显著提升了紫外光谱偏振技术对HWO任务的技术就绪度（TRL）。

Abstract: Pollux is a high-resolution spectropolarimeter proposed by a European consortium for the Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO). Its design covers a broad spectral range from the far-ultraviolet (FUV) to the near-infrared (97-1\,750 nm), with polarimetric channels relying on \ch{MgF2} birefringent optics in the mid- and near-UV (MUV-NUV), and on an innovative all-reflective polarimeter in the FUV, where no birefringent material is available. To validate these polarimeters, whose required polarimetric precision is $10^{-3}$, a dedicated vacuum ultraviolet test bench has been developed, with two configurations: one for the MUV-NUV range (120-290 nm) and one for the FUV range (98-120 nm). We present the first laboratory results obtained with this bench. On the MUV-NUV configuration, the full optical chain has been integrated: a first polarised spectrum of the deuterium lamp was acquired, the polarisation generation subsystem was validated against Mueller matrix predictions, and a first end-to-end polarimetric measurement was performed. On the FUV configuration, the windowless deuterium plasma source has been characterised, the alignment strategy of the K-mirror modulator has been implemented, and the mirror-based analyser has been manufactured and tested, showing a polarisation extinction ratio of 10 at 120 nm. These results demonstrate the operation of the bench and pave the way for the characterisation of the polarimetric precision of the Pollux polarimeters, increasing the Technology Readiness Level of UV spectropolarimetry for HWO.

</details>


### [152] [The Wetterstein Millimeter Telescope: A New German Facility for Astronomy and Geodesy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02194)
*Alexander Neidhardt,Matthias Kadler,A. K. -Baczko,Guido Dietl,Urs Hugentobler,Matthias Schartner,Tobias Ullmann,Till Rehm,Jompoj Wongphechauxsorn,for the WMT consortium*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: Wetterstein Millimeter Telescope (WMT) 是一个计划建于德国楚格峰的宽带（1.2–120 GHz）射电望远镜，旨在支持射电天文、大地测量VLBI、卫星通信、空间态势感知及技术开发，并融入国际VLBI网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建设一台多功能、宽带、高海拔射电望远镜，以增强德国及欧洲在射电天文与大地测量VLBI领域的观测能力，并支撑多学科应用需求。

Method: 通过德国资深研究机构合作，推进WMT天线设计优化与选址（楚格峰UFS站），并规划其与现有及未来国际VLBI网络（如EVN、GMVA、ngVLA、SKA-VLBI）的协同运行。

Result: 项目取得近期进展，包括天线设计演进与系统集成规划；WMT有望成为下一代VLBI关键节点，提升毫米波观测灵敏度与时空分辨率。

Conclusion: WMT不仅将填补欧洲高山毫米波VLBI观测空白，还将推动跨学科科研基础设施发展，强化德国在全球VLBI格局中的战略地位。

Abstract: The Wetterstein Millimeter Telescope (WMT) is a planned broadband (1.2-120 GHz) radio telescope to be established near the Environmental Research Station Schneefernerhaus (UFS) on Germany's highest mountain, the Zugspitze. Developed by a consortium of German research institutes and partners, the WMT is conceived as a multidisciplinary research infrastructure supporting radio astronomy, geodetic VLBI, satellite communications, space situational awareness, and technology development. The telescope is designed to operate within international VLBI networks, including the European VLBI Network, the Global mm-VLBI Array, and future ngVLA and SKA-VLBI observations. This contribution summarizes recent progress in the WMT project, including the evolution of the antenna design, and highlights the potential of the WMT to support future astronomical and geodetic VLBI.

</details>


### [153] [Spatial Mapping and Capacitor Trimming Developments to Improve Usable Pixel Yield in PRIMA FIRESS Kilo-Pixel Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02273)
*Chris Albert,Jonathon Hunacek,Hien Nguyen,Ritoban Basu Thakur,Andrew Beyer,Charles,Bradford,Peter Day,Pierre Echternacht,Byeong Ho Eom,Logan Foote,Marc Foote,Steve Hailey-Dunsheath,Reinier Janssen,Elijah Kane,Henry,LeDuc*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: PRIMA任务将使用8千像素KID阵列，本文提出了一种改进的谐振频率到空间位置映射系统，以在KID阵列转移至飞行外壳后仍保持其映射精度，从而支持天文观测并实现激光微调电容元件以优化频率分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保KID阵列在从标定装置转移到飞行外壳后仍能保持精确的频率-位置映射，以支持天文观测，并提升器件性能（如提高产率、降低串扰和频率漂移敏感性）。

Method: 设计并实现一种改进的谐振频率到空间位置映射系统，并结合激光微调技术对KID的电容元件进行优化调节。

Result: 成功维持了KID阵列的映射精度，实现了共振频率的优化分离，提升了工作产率、降低了串扰和频率漂移敏感性。

Conclusion: 该映射系统为PRIMA任务中KID阵列的高可靠性运行提供了关键技术支撑，有助于提升远红外天文观测性能。

Abstract: The Probe far-Infrared Mission for Astrophysics (PRIMA) will use 8 kilo-pixel kinetic inductance detector (KID) arrays in its spectrometer module. We present an improved resonant frequency to spatial position mapping system designed to preserve each array's mapping after transferring it from the mapping apparatus to the flight housing. Such a mapping is necessary for astronomical observations, and additionally allows us to laser trim the capacitive elements of KIDs to optimize resonance separation in frequency space. This increases the operating yield by eliminating collided resonances, reduces crosstalk, and reduces the sensitivity to frequency drift over time.

</details>


### [154] [Foundation Models for Astrophysics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02573)
*Xiaosheng Zhao,Yuan-Sen Ting*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基础模型（Foundation Models）的核心概念——可迁移表征（transferable representation），强调其在天文学等数据丰富但标签稀缺领域的潜力与挑战。文章指出，基础模型的关键不在于是否使用Transformer或大规模自监督预训练，而在于其学到的表征能否在新任务上实现少样本或零样本迁移。当前天文领域虽广泛采用类似架构，但跨仪器、种群和任务的稳健迁移仍罕见，反映出更广泛的物理科学中表征迁移的普遍困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决天文学中数据丰富但标注稀缺的问题，探索基础模型能否通过可迁移表征提升泛化能力，尤其在依赖仿真数据的场景下。

Method: 从第一性原理出发阐释‘可迁移表征’的概念，系统梳理支撑基础模型的关键要素：架构设计、自监督目标、规模扩展、适应方法及跨模态学习，并强调方法与目标（即表征可迁移性）之间的本质区分。

Result: 指出当前天文领域多数模型仅模仿基础模型架构，尚未充分验证跨任务、跨仪器、跨种群的表征迁移能力；稳健迁移在视觉与物理科学中整体仍属罕见，进一步扩展规模或重构表征理论可能是突破方向。

Conclusion: 基础模型的真正标志是表征的可迁移性而非技术组件；天文学应用尚处早期阶段，需以严谨实证检验迁移效果，未来进展应以跨域少/零样本性能提升为关键证据。

Abstract: Foundation models are high-capacity networks pretrained once on broad data and then reused across many tasks. This chapter introduces them through the idea of a transferable representation, the internal description a network forms during training, which, rather than the fitted task, is what carries over to new problems. We develop the idea from first principles for an astronomical reader, starting from why a representation matters and what makes one useful, and then surveying the architectures, self-supervised objectives, scaling, adaptation, and cross-modal learning that produce one. A theme throughout is the distinction between these methods and the goal they serve. The presence of a transformer, a self-supervised objective, and large-scale pretraining does not by itself make a model a foundation model, since the defining property is that the learned representation transfers, as tested by its ability to work on new tasks with little or no task-specific training data (few-shot and zero-shot learning). We then consider astronomy, where data are abundant but labels are scarce and simulations often stand in for ground truth. Here we offer a cautious reading of the current literature, in which many models adopt the architecture of foundation models while clear demonstrations of transfer across instruments, populations, and tasks remain comparatively rare. This is to be expected, since robust transfer beyond language is still uncommon even in vision and the wider physical sciences, and whether further scaling or a different account of representation will close the gap remains an open question. We close by placing the goal within the broader aim of machine intelligence and outlining the evidence that would mark real progress.

</details>


### [155] [CCAT: Optical Design of the 410 GHz Prime-Cam Module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02579)
*Tilak M. Patel,Tanner Buck,Anthony Huber,Eve M. Vavagiakis,James Burgoyne,Scott Chapman,Ben Keller,Jenna E. Moore,Michael D. Niemack,CCAT Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了Prime-Cam望远镜410 GHz宽带模块的光学性能，采用基于SO LATR设计的三透镜硅光学系统，利用Zemax和Huygens PSF分析评估波前质量、光束形状及像质指标，并通过容差分析检验其对装配误差的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 410 GHz工作波长更短，对波前质量和光束形状提出更高要求，需验证该频段下光学系统性能是否满足21,000像素极化敏感KID探测器的成像需求。

Method: 基于Ansys Zemax OpticStudio进行光学建模与Huygens PSF分析，评估不同模块位置下的Strehl比、椭圆度和环围能量；结合逆增量法与蒙特卡洛方法开展初步容差分析。

Result: 量化了410 GHz与350 GHz模块在像质指标（如Strehl比、椭圆度、环围能量）上的差异，并识别出关键敏感装配误差源。

Conclusion: 该三透镜硅光学设计在410 GHz下整体可行，但需严格控制特定对准误差以保障成像性能，为后续工程实现提供依据。

Abstract: Prime-Cam is a first-generation instrument for the Fred Young Submillimeter Telescope (FYST), enabling wide-field, multi-frequency observations for cosmology, line-intensity mapping, and galaxy studies. We present an optical performance study for a candidate 410 GHz broadband module designed to field approximately 21,000 polarisation-sensitive kinetic inductance detectors (KIDs). The three-lens silicon design was adapted from the SO LATR design and used for the existing 280 and 350 GHz Prime-Cam instrument modules, as well as this study. At 410 GHz, a shorter wavelength places tighter demands on wavefront quality and beam shape. Using Ansys Zemax OpticStudio and Huygens PSF analysis, we evaluate candidate module positions, compare 350 and 410 GHz performance, and assess field-dependent Strehl ratio, ellipticity, and encircled-energy behaviour. A preliminary tolerancing study, using inverse increment and Monte Carlo methods, tests sensitivity to selected alignment perturbations.

</details>


### [156] [SDSS-V Local Volume Mapper (LVM): Dithered Data Cube Reconstruction with 3dcubegen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02597)
*H. J. Ibarra-Medel,A. Z. Lugo-Aranda,R. de J. Zermeño,Sebastián F. Sánchez,Lesly Castañeda-Carlos,J. Eduardo Méndez-Delgado,K. Kreckel,Roeland P. van der Marel,R. Orozco-Duarte,Aida Wofford,Castalia Alenka Negrete,Irene Cruz-González,Carlos G. Román-Zúñiga,Guillermo A. Blanc,Evelyn J. Johnston,Ivan Katkov,Alfredo J. Mejía-Narváez,Tony Wong,Oleg V. Egorov*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了3DCubeGen，一种用于SDSS-V Local Volume Mapper (LVM)数据处理的灵活、可扩展的三维数据立方体重建工具，旨在高效合并多次dither观测的光谱数据，提升信噪比、空间分辨率和对微弱发射特征的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDSS-V LVM开展大视场积分场光谱巡天，需将多次dither观测的行堆叠光谱（RSS）稳健地重建为均匀三维数据立方体，以支撑科学分析。

Method: 提出3DCubeGen工具，支持多dither数据的通量守恒合并与误差传播，借鉴并拓展CALIFA等巡天已有方法，具备处理海量光谱（百万量级）的能力。

Result: 成功应用于LMC、SMC及近邻星系等大量LVM观测，生成的数据立方体显著改善空间采样与光谱深度，支持电离气体、恒星种群与动力学的详细研究。

Conclusion: 3DCubeGen是LVM数据处理的关键工具，为SDSS-V Local Volume Mapper的科学产出提供了坚实的技术支撑。

Abstract: The Sloan Digital Sky Survey V (SDSS-V) Local Volume Mapper (LVM) is conducting an unprecedented wide-field integral field spectroscopic survey of the Milky Way, the Magellanic Clouds, and nearby galaxies using a strategy based on multiple dithered observations to achieve full spatial coverage, improved spatial sampling, and enhanced spectral depth. However, the scientific exploitation of these observations requires a robust methodology to combine the individual row-stacked spectra (RSS) into homogeneous three-dimensional data cubes. In this work, we present 3DCubeGen, a flexible and scalable reconstruction tool designed to combine multiple LVM dithers while preserving flux and propagating uncertainties. The method enables the coaddition of large datasets, improving the signal-to-noise ratio, enhancing spatial resolution, and increasing sensitivity to faint emission features, following and extending approaches previously implemented in integral field surveys such as CALIFA. We apply 3DCubeGen to a large set of LVM observations, including the Large and Small Magellanic Clouds and nearby galaxies, combining thousands of dithers corresponding to millions of spectra. The resulting data products demonstrate significant improvements in spatial sampling and spectral depth, enabling detailed studies of the ionised gas, stellar populations, and kinematics across extended regions. 3DCubeGen provides a robust and scalable solution for LVM data cube reconstruction and represents a key tool for exploiting the scientific potential of the SDSS-V Local Volume Mapper.

</details>
